**Stage 1 (`wave55-pre`), step by step.**
0. **Before the run:** `beforeEveryDeviceRun` (rendered above; executable verbatim from any directory, as its note says:
   round 3, verify M1 — its prose moved to `beforeEveryDeviceRunNote`, `plan-build.py` asserts `bash -n` on it, and
   `fix-r3.guard-proof.out.txt` runs it). Its first clause
   refuses a tree whose code differs from HEAD, untracked files included (round 1, S9; round 2, S1); its second refuses
   under 20 GiB free (S5). Set `ANDROID_SERIAL` when more than one device is attached (round-1 N3; the host's emulators may
   be shared).
1. **Run** the `stage1Probe.cmd` (≈ 22 min at the quiet-host rate of 2.5 min/section: `briefs-audit.md` §6). **The hash
   read** `stage1Probe.hashRead` (round 1, M2; round 2, S10): its BEFORE part (the launch count) runs before the launch,
   its AFTER part in parallel with it — one-shot per launch, right after `provision rc=0`, while the devices are up. A
   `--resume` is a new launch and gets its own read. Then commit `../wave55-gate/build-hashes.txt` with named paths before
   any later device run.
2. **Read**, in the `readOut` order, base first, each into its record under `wave55-gate/stage1/wave55-pre/` (round 1, S2:
   `records`): `abDiff`; `score` → `floors` (`probe-readout.mjs --stage1 --sections`); `controls`; `geometry`. Then
   `node tools/titan/results/wave52-gate/cells.mjs '<lane terms>' wave55-open wave55-pre` per lane.
3. **OPEN the pictures** before any label:
   - hyphenate-character-001…004 ios (and hyphens-vertical-001 ios: byte-identical, D22);
   - nested-clip-3 and its siblings, android;
   - dir-style-02a android: rows 6 / 7 / 9 must read from x16;
   - bidi-tab-001 android and dynamic-offset-rtl-001 android;
   - inset-005 / 006 / 011 / 014 on both natives;
   - counter-suffix ×3 and -005 ×2: iOS rows 3–4 `א. foo`, rows 5–6 flush, row 9's tabular `1`;
   - clip-rect android and flex safe-001 android.
4. **Decide.** Each lane decides by its `stage1Decision` (the facts blocks of §2), on its own keys and cells only. Another
   lane's carrier movement is never a reason (C2, rule 6x). Nothing is committed yet: step 5 first.
5. **The L4 probe-time arm `drop-L4-U2`, FIRST** (round 1, S5: before any stage-1 revert or swap commit, so HEAD is still the
   `wave55-pre` tree and the arm's Δ is U2's alone; `ab-arms55.sh` refuses an `--against` run whose hash block names a tree
   that differs from HEAD in code).
   - Before the window, commit the gate records with named paths (`build-hashes.txt`, the step-2 records): the runner
     refuses a tree with tracked changes (round 1, S6). Never `checkout -f` or a directory-wide `git add` to get there.
   - The arms file is built from `abArms` `drop-L4-U2`'s `drops`: every `wave55 L4 U2-<p>-S1…` fix commit and every unit
     it names, resolved from `git log` and listed in `git log` ORDER, newest first (round 2, process-holes M1: `drops`
     gives the set; the relative order of `U2-ios-S1` and `U2-android-S1` is the order S1 landed them, and the runner
     refuses a sha that is not older than the sha before it): `drop-L4-U2 css-text-decor ios,android <sha…>`. Run
     `bash tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/ab-arms55.sh <arms-file> --dry-run --against wave55-pre` (round-2 N3: the dry
     run then reads the same tree check as the run), then `--run --against wave55-pre` (D9). The runner reverse-applies a
     non-chain list cumulatively in a private export (round 2, M1; `../wave55-gate/ab-arms55.revert-check-proof.out.txt`),
     so the `-S1` fixes on U2 no longer make the arm unrunnable. It refuses to run on bytes whose `--run` self-test record
     cites other bytes (§8 step 6, S6).
   - The runner's read-out is ARM first, `ab-diff.mjs wave55-ab-drop-L4-U2 wave55-pre --threshold 0.002 --platforms ios,android`
     → `wave55-gate/final/ab-drop-L4-U2.txt` (the runner's fixed path; a stage-1 record by content, round-1 N5 queued).
     With it: `node tools/titan/results/wave52-gate/cells.mjs 'text-decoration-inset-005|text-decoration-inset-006|text-decoration-inset-011|text-decoration-inset-014' wave55-open wave55-ab-drop-L4-U2 wave55-pre`.
   - Expect arm M (`abArms`): every native row up and f. A miss is read for magnitude and named. It reverts nothing by
     itself; rule 4 on `[M]` at stage 1 already decided U1.
6. **Revert, then regenerate.**
   - Commit the revert (named paths).
   - APPEND `{lane, unit, run: 'wave55-pre', commit, revertCommit, rule}` to `probe-decisions/reverted.json` (D25; never a
     new file, never a rewrite: `plan-build.py` refuses a regeneration that drops a recorded row). One row per UNIT: its
     `-S1` fix commits go in that row's `commit` (round-2 N1; a row naming `U2-S1` is refused by name). Regenerate the whole
     chain (round 2, process-holes S8): `plan-build.py`; `plan-prose.py --fill` and `--check`;
     `../wave55-gate/final55-analysis.gen.py` then `--check` (the generated read-out refuses a foreign expectations sha256);
     `../wave55-gate/readers-calibration.sh` in a throwaway worktree. All of it in ONE commit with the decision file.
   - Re-read `wave55-pre` with the regenerated expectations, on the score side AND with `control-check.mjs`, into
     `wave55-gate/stage1/wave55-pre/` (`*.regen.*` names). Record the expected failure count, and list the withdrawn
     carriers that did NOT move as unproven (wave-54 lesson "A probe revert must withdraw its carriers into must-not-move").
     An L6 U2 revert also withdraws the may-move class and the `drop-L6-U2` arm (D26).
7. **Swaps and re-cuts the briefs pre-registered** (NB-ios-b; L5's U1 LRM fallback). Both are pre-registered
   `contingencyUnits` (round 2, process-holes S2: L5's `U1-ios-LRM` now has its generator encoding and its landing row).
   Each lands as its own commit with `land-units.sh --contingency <L>:<U>` (`L1:NB-ios-b` or `L5:U1-ios-LRM`), then an
   appended `probe-decisions/contingency.json` row and the step-6 regeneration chain: the landed unit is prepended to every
   revertOrder that holds its base unit. It is re-probed as a pre-registered `stage1Probe.reprobes` id (`wave55-pre-r1`,
   `-r2`) over its lane's stage-1 sections, with its own hash read and its own records directory (D13, round 1 M2 / S2).
8. **The L4 face tolerance (round 2, instrument-teeth S1, D33).** Every face-present `[M]` / `[F]` native line prints `Δx1`.
   Set `X1_TOL` in `ua-heading-margin.geometry.py` to max |Δx1| + 2 over the device lines (today ±12, measured on WEB's
   face only), as a probe edit committed with named paths plus the step-6 regeneration chain, recorded in §10, BEFORE any
   decision on the face keys and before stage 2. `probes-teeth-r2.py` then pre-registers the clip-10 fake FAIL wherever the
   new tolerance allows it; otherwise §9 D33 keeps it as the named blind spot. **Round 3 (`fix-r3.done.md`):** the same
   edit re-sets `FACE_INK_TOL` (the face-present bands' dark px against the ref's, today ±6 % on web's face: −1.8 … +1.3 %)
   to max |Δ %| + 2 over the device lines — each face-present line prints `ink b<k>:<n>/<ref n>c<Δ %>` — and checks the
   component counts (`<n>/<ref n>`) are equal on every device line; a device line whose only reason is the
   `ink:` / `mass:` / `core:` / `face ink:` read of round 3 is read the same way (a measured tolerance, never widened to
   pass a fake of `probes-teeth-r3.py`). **Round 4 (`fix-r4.done.md`, D39):** a face-present native line also prints
   `xor b<k>:<n>` (its bands against the face reference, XOR ≤ `FACE_XOR_TOL` 2) and the L5 `[H]` / `[T]` native lines print
   `ink <n> vs <twin>`. A device 2em face sits 1768–2510 px level-XOR from Chromium's (`fix-r4.measure.out.txt` §2, the
   inset-001…004 anchors the natives already paint), and an iOS tabular '1' is not Android's, so these reads are NOT re-set
   to the device: a device line whose only reason is `face xor:` or an `[H]` / `[T]` twin `ink:` is decided by a recorded
   look (the line against its C arm / ok-all model, written in §10), and the same probe edit sets
   `FACE_RUN_DEFAULT = 'wave55-pre'` in `ua-heading-margin.geometry.py` and `TWIN_RUN_DEFAULT = 'wave55-pre'` in
   `counter-suffix-rows.geometry.py` (dry runs: `--face-run` / `--twin-run`). From `wave55-probe` on, those keys read the
   decided device pixels, XOR ≤ 2 (at `wave55-pre` itself the read compares the run with itself: the look is the decision).
   A unit reverted at stage 1 leaves its default `None`. `FACE_XOR_TOL` and `INK_TOL` are never widened to pass a fake of
   `probes-teeth-r4.py`.

**Stage 2 (`wave55-probe`).** It runs after the stage-1 decisions are committed and regenerated.
- ≈ 65–80 min on a quiet host. Wave 54 planned ≈ 65 min for the same section count (`probeRun.sections`); its run took 07:27 → 08:4x UTC
  (`wave54-gate/_note.md` "Stage 2").
- Read per `probeRun.readOut`, base first.
- Before the run, `beforeEveryDeviceRun`; around it, `probeRun.hashRead` (round 1, M2; round 2, S10: its BEFORE part
  before the launch), committed with named paths after.
- Every commit that trips rules 1–8 is reverted, with the same appended-row regeneration chain (step 6: `plan-build.py`,
  `plan-prose.py`, `final55-analysis.gen.py` + `--check`, `readers-calibration.sh` in a throwaway) and re-read as at
  stage 1, the records under `wave55-gate/stage2/wave55-probe/`.
- css-color, css-pseudo, css-sizing and css-ui are not probed (`notProbed`), each with its replacement. One key is first
  read at the gate (`keysFirstReadAtTheGate`).

**What each lane must show at stage 1.** Generated (round 1, S7: the hand summary carried counts that drift): for each
lane, the keys stage 1 reads by class and the keys first read at stage 2, counted from `geometryProbes` × `stage1Probe.sections`,
and its `stage1Decision` verbatim — the record a miss is read against.

**Revert rules.** Wave 54 §6 verbatim, with the wave-54 §10 amendments and the wave-55 instances folded in; amendments are
marked. "Read `wave54-open` → the probe (same host). The unit of revert is the **commit**. A commit is reverted out of the
integrated tree when ANY of these holds for a cell or capture it carries" — read `wave55-open` for `wave54-open`.

1. **Lost at the probe.** "A carrier cell (any lane, any tier, predicted or not) is P on `wave54-open` and f on the probe.
   This is R1's empty lost list applied at the probe."
2. **Moved down.** "Δ ≤ −0.002 on any prediction row whose `direction` is `up-or-stay`, at any tier."
   - **Amended for wave 55 (D5).** One row forecasts a fall: a `down-preregistered` row fires at
     Δ ≤ predictedDelta − 0.002.
   - Rows "whose sign is not pre-registered (`direction: "undirected"`, each with its `directionWhy`)" — three this wave,
     per `plan-build.out.txt` — "are **exempt from rule 2** … A fall on one is looked at against the ref and named with its
     cause in the probe read-out and the PR (`probe-readout.mjs` prints it as `READ`); it reverts nothing by itself. Rules 1
     and 5 still bind them."
   - "A row DEMOTED by a probe decision becomes undirected too. Same-host noise is 0 on every platform."
3. **Below floor.** "A gating prediction … scores below its `floor`. Read by `probe-readout.mjs` over the `--movers 0` probe
   record (a gating cell that did not move is READ and fails). A row demoted by a probe decision has no floor."
   - **Amended (wave-54 §10 item 7 ¶2, and the lesson it became).** Every gating row floors at 0.95, the pass threshold,
     never a replay magnitude. This wave's count (`plan-build.out.txt` "gating predictions") includes MED rows that carry a
     gating geometry key. An f→f row has `floorNA` (C11).
   - A RESTATE clause such as "must not fall below probe − 0.002" is claimed only if `adjudicate.mjs` reads it.
4. **Wrong geometry.** "A gating key of `geometry-gate.py` is not PASS (FAIL: exit 1, the runner names the unit;
   UNMEASURED: exit 3, re-run its section — never a pass)."
   - **Amended (wave-54 §10 item 7 ¶1; lesson "Rule 4 reverts a unit that improves a picture but leaves a geometry
     source").** GEOMETRY WRONG on a gating key reverts the unit even when the score rose and the fail ink vanished. The
     residue is queued with the probe picture.
   - **Amended (wave-54 §10 item 7 ¶3; lesson "A control pinned on the base picture's WRONG line").** A control on a
     registered carrier pins the TARGET picture or is report-only (C4 re-classed the wave-54 keys the wave-55 units break
     by design).
   - **New (C15).** `--sections` prints the keys of unprobed sections as NOT PROBED.
5. **Leak.** "A composed capture outside every carrier set differs in decoded pixels (R4), or a per-test IR document outside
   the wire carriers differs in bytes and is not 'renumbered' (R4b). It is bisected to a commit, one re-probe of the leaked
   section per step; never guessed."
   - **Wave 55.** The capture must also be outside L6 `mayMove`. A may-move change is not a leak: its score keeps the
     0.002 band, beyond which it is a look-first leak signal naming L6 U2.
   - It must also be outside the ring-fence, which is reported plainly.
   - There is no wire carrier this wave.
6. **The unit is the commit.** "(`lanes.*.revertUnits`; `plan-build.py` asserts that each lane's units cover its carriers
   exactly). A cell carried by several commits of one lane reverts them in `revertOrder` (latest first), re-probing its
   sections between steps."
   - The wave-55 dependencies are rule 6 of the block below; 6x covers the shared stems (C2).
   - **Amended (wave-54 §10 item 6 ¶4).** A fix commit on a landed unit (`-S1`) reverts before its unit.
   - **Amended (wave-54 lesson "A probe revert must withdraw its carriers into must-not-move").** Every revert is entered
     as an appended row of `probe-decisions/reverted.json` (D25) and regenerated. The probe is re-read with the regenerated expectations on the score side AND with
     `control-check.mjs`, and the closing gate must read 0 on exactly the withdrawn carriers.
7. **Stage 1.** Wave 54: "decides L1 and L2". **Wave 55:** it decides every lane (`stage1Probe.decides`; C1).
8. Wave 54: "**U3b is probe-decided**". **Wave 55:** the contingency rule. L1 NB-ios-b lands only on its trigger; L4
   GO-SMALL (`probe-decisions/holds.json` `goSmall.held`, D25; `--go-small` only for dry runs) holds U2-android (D1). No unit is
   probe-decided this wave.

**Tier** (wave 54, verbatim). "Confidence decides only whether a cell has a floor and a geometry trigger. A MED or lower
cell that rises less than predicted, or not at all, is read and labelled, never a trigger. Rules 1 and 5 apply to every row
of every tier and rule 2 to every `up-or-stay` row of every tier." **Amended:** every row that carries a gating geometry key
floors at 0.95, whatever its tier. "**The closing gate runs only on a tree whose probes hold.**"

The machine form that `probe-readout.mjs` and the decisions read (`expectations.json` `revertRule`):

<!-- gen:revert-rule (plan-prose.py; do not edit by hand) -->
- **1** lost: a carrier cell (any lane, any tier, predicted or not) that is P on wave55-open and f on the probe reverts the commit that carries it
- **2** moved down: Δ <= -0.002 (base → probe) on any prediction row whose direction is "up-or-stay" reverts the commit that carries it; a "down-preregistered" row fires at Δ <= predictedDelta − 0.002 (D5); an "undirected" row is EXEMPT (looked at, named with its cause, never a revert by itself). Rule 2 is read AT THE PROBES (probe-readout.mjs); at the closing gate adjudicate.mjs enforces the gating floors (R4) and must-not-move (R5) only
- **3** floor: a gating prediction below its floor (0.95, every gating row) or not the predicted verdict reverts the commit that carries it; a row demoted by a probe decision has no floor; an f→f row has floorNA (C11)
- **4** geometry: a geometryProbes gating key whose line does not end in its exact expect string (or lacks its contains string) reverts the unit geometry-gate.py names; an UNMEASURED gating key of a probed section is not a pass (exit 3: re-run the section)
- **5** leak: a composed capture outside every carrier set, outside L6 mayMove and outside the ring-fence that differs in decoded pixels (R4), or a per-test IR document that differs in bytes and is not "renumbered" (R4b: no wire carrier this wave), reverts the commit it is bisected to
- **6** unit: the commit (lanes.*.revertUnits); a cell carried by several commits of one lane reverts them in revertOrder (latest first), re-probing between steps. Dependencies: L1 NB-ios before the wave-54 U3b (never U3b alone); L2 U2 ↔ U1 a pair; L3 U2 before U1; L4 U2-<p> before U1-<p>; L5 D before U2o (its SUFFIX NOTE is true only with U2o: round-1 S1), U2i before U2o; L6 U2 before U1. Every rule-6 state is compiled and unit-tested by its lane before stage 1 (round-1 S12: revertStatesCompiled in the lane note)
- **6x** shared stems (C2): carriers are disjoint by construction; L6's raw may-move set holds 12 stems that are L2 / L3 carriers (lanes.L6-android-paint-order.sharedStems) — a move there is the carrier lane's, attributed by the drop-L6-U2 arm; never an L6 revert by itself
- **7** stage 1: every lane is decided at wave55-pre by its stage1Decision on the stage-1 sections; a unit reverted there is withdrawn (probeDecisions) before wave55-probe
- **8** contingency: L1 NB-ios-b and L5 U1-ios-LRM land only on their triggers (lanes.*.contingencyUnits; an appended probe-decisions/contingency.json row; land-units.sh --contingency <L>:<U>); L4 GO-SMALL (probe-decisions/holds.json goSmall.held) holds U2-android and is decided BEFORE the landing only — after the landing the same outcome is a rule-4 revert row of U2-android, never holds.json (round 2, process-holes S7)
- **tier:** a MED or lower row without a gating key carries no floor; a shortfall against its magnitude is read and labelled. Rules 1 and 5 bind every row; rule 2 every up-or-stay / down-preregistered row
<!-- /gen:revert-rule -->

## 8. Risks, and the order of operations (the closing procedure step by step)

**Risks.**
1. **Code first executed on device.** None of it can run on the JVM or Catalyst in its rendered form, so stage 1 and the
   geometry rows are the evidence:
   - L1 NB-ios's line-box snapping (R1: the NB-ios-b trigger);
   - L2 U2's oversized in-flow placeable under #outer's clip and the backdrop two-pass;
   - L3 U1's mount under host-inactive composed captures, and U2's RTL origin;
   - L4's margin on both natives and the restored face (iOS wrap of a folded paragraph);
   - L5's isolate controls in CoreText, and `monospacedDigit` on the bundled Inter;
   - L6's behind-content band raster parity, corpus-wide on Android (the may-move stems: §2 union block).
2. **Partial landings that are pre-registered losses or wrong pictures.** All are excluded by `revertOrder` and §4:
   - L2 U1 without U2 (the refused clip-3 picture), or U2 without U1 (worse than the base);
   - L3 PLAIN;
   - the wave-54 U3b reverted under L1 NB-ios (replay Rd: -004 ios P → f);
   - L4 U2 without U1 (the B-arm falls).
3. **The seam files.** The hunks of §3's two tables (`landing-manifest.json` `units[].seams`) from five lanes. Mitigation:
   anchors re-read; `SeamReachabilityTest.kt`'s writers at their pinned anchors (round 1, M1); `land-units.sh --check`
   (each patch alone, the cumulative series, and the export-tree revert dry-runs); a re-cut by the lane on any failure;
   focused suites, and every revert state compiled (round 1, S12), on the lane's tree.
4. **Host load from parallel lanes.** Compose `:runtime` is compiled by L2, L3, L4, L5 and L6 (+ L7 U6-compose), and
   SwiftUI by L1, L4 and L5 (+ L7 U6-swift), each lane in its own worktree (D18): CPU and memory contention, no shared
   files. Mitigation: `nice`; retry on locks; never touch a foreign file. The single-writer sweep on the landed tree is
   the count of record, and `beforeEveryDeviceRun` stops the daemons of every tree on a `TREES:` line.
5. **A corpus-wide Android paint change (L6 U2) and an extract-route change (L7 U5).** Covered by the may-move band, the
   `[ID]` rows, the fixture net (R8) and [W-L7a].
6. **Host.** It is the host of `wave55-open` (`../wave55-gate/_note.md` preflight).
   - No OS, Xcode, simulator-runtime, emulator-image or Chrome-for-Testing update may happen between `wave55-open` and
     `wave55-final`.
   - Swap was 9.4 / 10 GB at the opening launch. Check `sysctl vm.swapusage` and load before every launch;
     css-view-transitions may need `--resume` under swap.
7. **Blast-radius under-reporting** (the dangerous direction). Every census is re-derived by its skeptic with its own
   script; D2 was found at generation, D21 (the 3-dp medium default, logical sides and shorthands: may-move +26
   documents) and D22 (the vertical run NB-ios would have pinned) by the plan skeptics; the S1 combined-tree skeptic
   replays all seven.
8. **The fixture net first meets L6 U2's band change at the closing gate** (round-1 N4, accepted). D11 keeps `test-all.sh`
   off the probes; `android-paint-order.gate-fixtures-scan.out.txt` finds few bordered gate components with a child. A red
   net (R8) costs a re-gate after its cause is found in the picture.

**Order of operations.**
1. **`wave55-open` is finished, scored and DISCHARGED.**
   - The plan checks of record already exist: the watchlist over `wave55-open` (unmatched 0) and the reader calibrations.
   - Any later divergence is written into §10 BEFORE any lane lands.
2. **Commit the plan** with named paths, once every planning stage's done note says COMPLETE.
   - **First, the regeneration of record** (round 1, S14 — the owed D8 / D9 / D20 strings are applied by it, D28):
     `plan-build.py` → `expectations.json`, `watchlist.txt`, `plan-build.out.txt`; `plan-prose.py --fill` then `--check`;
     `../wave55-gate/final55-analysis.gen.py` then `--check`; then, in ONE throwaway worktree, `probes-selfcheck.py`,
     `readers-selfcheck.sh` and `../wave55-gate/readers-calibration.sh` (they cite the expectations sha256). The fix pass
     of round 1 ran exactly this (`fix-r1.done.md`); any later regeneration repeats it.
   - The files:
     - `PLAN.md`, `plan-prose.py`, `plan-prose.selfcheck.sh`, `plan-prose.out.txt`, `plan.done.md`;
     - the generator's untracked outputs and its committed decision inputs `probe-decisions/*.json` (D25);
     - the round-1 instruments: `probes-teeth.py`, the wave-55 `compose-wpt-content-box-cb.geometry.py`,
       `android-paint-order.maymove-medium.{py,out.txt}`, the skeptic reports and `fix-r1.done.md`;
     - the landing stage's `land-units.sh`, its self-test, its outputs and `landing-manifest.json`;
     - the `wave55-gate/` readers, their calibration, the toolkit proofs, `ab-arms55.*` (L7 U3, optional: D19),
       `make-snapshot55.mjs` and `final55-analysis.{gen.py,sh}`.

   `plan-prose.py --check` and `final55-analysis.gen.py --check` must exit 0 on the committed tree.
3. **Builder lanes L1–L7.**
   - One Workflow, model opus, file-driven and resumable through `args.done` / `args.only`.
   - Each lane works in its own worktree and delivers patches (D18), provisioned per §0 (round 1, S10), named
     `<scratchpad>/wave55-<role>-<lane>` and created with `git worktree add --detach … HEAD`, never Workflow-isolated
     (round 2, S4).
   - **Resume rule (round 1, process-holes M3; round 2, S12).** A relaunched lane re-opens the worktree on its `TREES:` line
     (or the `git worktree list` entry under its `wave55-<role>-<lane>` name); runs `git status` / `git diff` there on its
     owned paths; restores every `MUTATING <file> sha256 <pre>` line that has no `RESTORED` pair (the mutation record is
     written BEFORE the edit, §0); deletes EVERY patch in its lane dir and re-cuts all of them; re-runs its focused suites
     and every revert state on the re-cut patches; rewrites the `patch:` lines; only then writes `STATUS:`.
     `land-units.sh --check` refuses a lane that skipped this.
   - **Fix lanes (round 2, S12).** A fix lane writes `STATUS: PARTIAL — fix-r<n> in progress` to the lane note BEFORE it
     touches the lane dir, and `STATUS: COMPLETE` LAST — after its re-cut `patch:` lines, its focused suites and
     `revertStatesCompiled:`. `land-units.sh` reads the last `STATUS:` line, so a fix lane killed half-way never lands
     under the builder's COMPLETE; it resumes by the rule above.
   - Skeptics re-derive each census with their own script, on the lane's patches applied to a throwaway worktree; fix
     lanes follow.
   - Lanes pause during any device step.
4. **Plan-level check before landing.**
   - Every `<run> <sec>/<test> <platform> <P|f> <ssim>` citation in the lane notes is re-resolved.
   - A lane hand-off that changes a carrier, a prediction or a unit regenerates `expectations.json` through
     `plan-build.py`, then runs `plan-prose.py --fill` and `--check`, all in the same commit.
5. **[W-L7a]** (D12), before the landing: Chromium plus converter, on throwaway worktrees, with no gate running.
   - pre = HEAD, post = HEAD + `unit-U5.patch` (§2 L7).
   - **Which differential runs (round 1, S13).** `wire-differential.sh` exists only in `unit-U2.patch`, so it runs from a
     THIRD throwaway at HEAD + `unit-U2.patch`; the [W-L7a] record names the sha256 of both `unit-U2.patch` and
     `unit-U5.patch`. Any later change to either patch's bytes (a fix, an `-S1`) re-runs [W-L7a] before stage 1.
   - Its result decides whether L7 U5 lands at its manifest place or under `--hold L7:U5`.
6. **Integration** (§4 step 4).
   - **First, the destructive-path self-tests on the CURRENT bytes (round 2, process-holes S6).**
     `bash tools/titan/results/wave55-plan/land-units.selftest.sh <scratch>` (sections A–G: the real landing path, the fault
     knob, `--from`, the records commit, the note guards, the pinned anchors, `--contingency <L:U>`) and
     `bash tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/ab-arms55.selftest.sh <scratch>` (`--run`, `checkout -B`, `revert`,
     `checkout -f`, records after the restore, T5l / T5m and the mutants). Each record's header must cite the sha256 of the
     script that will run (`shasum -a 256`); both records are committed with named paths BEFORE the landing. The stage-1 L4
     arm does not run on a runner whose `--run` self-test record cites other bytes.
   - `land-units.sh --check`: first with `--root` on a throwaway worktree — after copying the seven lane dirs into it (they
     are untracked files of the shared tree; round-1 N1) — then on the shared tree.
   - Then `land-units.sh` on the idle host (its refusals: §4).
   - Its stdout goes to `$TMPDIR` and is copied into `wave55-gate/` AFTER the run (records after the tree is final).
7. **The single-writer sweep** (§4 step 5), the docs restamp, and `doc-staleness-check.sh`.
8. **S1, the combined-tree skeptic.** It mutates ONLY in a throwaway worktree off the landed HEAD, never on the shared tree
   (round 1, S9: the next device run would build a killed mutation; `beforeEveryDeviceRun` now refuses a dirty tree). It
   runs:
   - the seam-hunk audit against §3;
   - the sweep counts against the doc tables;
   - git hygiene, including `git ls-files` for every cited evidence file;
   - the pointer audit;
   - a census replay per lane;
   - two mutations per lane;
   - `plan-prose.py --check`.

   - every revert state of every lane, compiled and focused-tested on the landed tree (round 1, S12).

   Fix lanes take its must-fix items, one `-S1` commit per touched unit. **The fix model (round 1, S9):** a fix lane
   delivers `fix-S1-<U>.patch` into its lane dir (with its `patch:` line; `land-units.sh --check` and `--contingency` accept
   it for a unit U of that lane, and still refuse it without the line — round 2, process-holes S3); the orchestrator lands
   each with `git apply --index --check`, `git apply --index` and a named-path commit `wave55 <L> <U>-S1: …`, in
   revertOrder-reverse order. An `-S1` commit reverts before its unit, and the A/B arms drop it first (`abArms[].drops`;
   the runner checks the arm's reverses cumulatively, round 2 M1).

   8½. **The sweep again** (round 1, S4): after the `-S1` commits, the full single-writer sweep (`--rerun-tasks`) and the
   restamp → `wave55-gate/sweep-landed.post-s1.txt`. Wave 54 needed three sweeps (its CBB revert took Compose
   3492 → 3486).

   9½. **Clean up the throwaways** (round 2, process-holes S5): after the landing's records commit and S1, every
   `wave55-*` lane, skeptic, fix and S1 worktree is removed (`git worktree remove --force <wt>`) and its DerivedData with
   it (`<wt>/.dd`); the `TREES:` lines stay as history. `beforeEveryDeviceRun`'s disk floor (20 GiB) is the backstop.
9. **Before EVERY device run:** `beforeEveryDeviceRun` (§7 block). That covers stage 1, its re-probes and its arm, stage 2,
   both closing launches, the closing arms and [W1].
10. **Stage 1 `wave55-pre`** (§7).
11. **Stage 2 `wave55-probe`** (§7).

   11½. **The sweep of the gate tree** (round 1, S4): after the LAST probe decision (every revert committed and
   regenerated), the full sweep, the restamp and `doc-staleness-check.sh` on exactly the tree the closing gate will run →
   `wave55-gate/sweep-landed.txt`. The gate tree then equals the ship tree code-wise.
12. **The closing gate `wave55-final`**, on a quiet host, in two launches. Each runs with `run_in_background` and
    `timeout: 7200000`. Wait for the task notification; no polling, no heartbeat. Set `ANDROID_SERIAL` when more than one
    device is attached, here and in every arm window (round-2 N11; the run refuses with exit 2 otherwise).
    1. `tools/titan/gate-driver.sh wave55-final --skip-fixture-net` (the corpus).
    2. **The hash of record**, right after `provision rc=0` of THIS launch:
       - wait with `hashRead`'s per-launch form on `tools/titan/runs/wave55-final/gate-driver/driver.log` (its BEFORE part
         run before the launch; round 2, S10);
       - run `tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/installed-build-hash.sh "wave55-final corpus launch (closing gate, launch k)"`;
       - built == installed must hold on both platforms;
       - the iOS witness is the probe's digest (`probeRun.hashRead`'s block, round 1 M2) when no Swift source changed
         between `wave55-probe` and the gate tree AND the block's `asset sha1` line is the same; the Android witness is the
         probe's apk sha1 when no Compose source changed and the `asset sha1` line is the same (round-1 N7; round 2, S9:
         BOTH bundles embed the last test-all IR, so a digest compares across launches only with the same IR);
       - the wait is per LAUNCH (round 2, S10): use `probeRun.hashRead`'s form with `wave55-final` (count the launches
         before this one, wait for a new `attempt N starting`, at most 1800 s), and do the same for a `--resume`.
    3. Verify the capture counts per completed section from the driver's summary: 30 sections, every column 48/48/48,
       css-cascade 43/43/43.
    4. `rm -f /tmp/titan-device-pool/provisioned-*`, then `tools/titan/gate-driver.sh wave55-final --skip-corpus` (the
       fixture net). Its hash read is annotated as a net-launch read (the race), and a post-net re-read is taken while the
       devices are up.
    5. **The tracked records before any arm window** (round 1, S6). Record `git diff --stat tools/visual/baseline-stats.json`
       (the net regenerates it); restore that one path (`git restore --source=HEAD -- tools/visual/baseline-stats.json`)
       only when the diff is timestamp-only, as `wave55-open` did; then commit `wave55-gate/build-hashes.txt` and the
       read-outs with named paths. `ab-arms55.sh` refuses a tree with tracked changes; never `checkout -f`, never a
       directory-wide `git add`.
13. **Read-out, in this order:** `bash tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/final55-analysis.sh`. It is GENERATED at planning
    (round 1, S11) by `final55-analysis.gen.py` from the pre-registered `expectations.json` strings (§6 block), with the
    prediction terms emitted from the rows, `--check` failing on drift, and its last line the verdict (exit 0 only when R6,
    adjudicate and the union control hold). It is proven RED on the identity record and HOLDS on the all-met synthetic
    (`readers-calibration.txt` F1–F2). Its steps:
    1. sections status;
    2. `scoreOfRecord` → `score-final.json`;
    3. `scoreReadout` → `score-final.movers0005.json` / `score-final.txt`;
    4. `geometryGate.closingCmd` → `geometry-final.json` / `.out.txt` (R6 is its exit);
    5. `adjudicate` → `adjudicate-final.txt`: R1, R2, R3, the floors, R7 with the complement, and the R6 retirement
       read-out. It exits 2 on any other record.
    6. `node tools/titan/results/wave55-plan/probe-readout.mjs tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/score-final.json`: rules 1–3
       and the leak signals over `probeRun.sections`, its default (adjudicate R5 and the control cover `probeRun.notProbed`).
       0 expected, and exactly 0 on any withdrawn carriers.
    7. `node tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/control-check.mjs wave55-open wave55-final --json tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/control-final-union.json`
       (R4 / R4b, with the may-move and ring-fence classes).
    8. The probe → final differential:
       `node tools/titan/results/wave52-gate/ab-diff.mjs wave55-probe wave55-final --threshold 0.002`. Only the carriers of
       units reverted after `wave55-probe` may differ.
    9. Review sheets (gained / lost / movers from the 0.005 record): `tools/titan/results/wave52-gate/review-sheets.mjs`.
    10. `node tools/titan/red-square-census.mjs wave55-final`.
    11. Every prediction row, open → final (`cells.mjs` over the rows' terms).
    12. The picture-correct tally (`pictureCorrectTally` against `geometry-final.json`); the DEGENERATE retirements
        (counter-suffix by hand from its keys, inset-005 / -006 ios by adjudicate R6); and
        `RUN=wave55-final WATCH=… watchlist-check.mjs` → unmatched 0.
14. **Cell review.**
    - Every flipped cell and every P→P target or mover is looked at against the frozen ref, at 2× where needed →
      `final/cell-review.json`.
    - An **adversarial second reader** takes every non-FAITHFUL label, every pass under 0.975, every carrier a control FAIL
      sits on, and every claimed tally entry → `final/cell-review.second-reader.json`.
    - The ring-fenced test is reported with its number.
    - R8 (fixture net exit 0 ×9) and R9 (the tooling suite on the ship tree).
15. **A/B arms**, after `beforeEveryDeviceRun` and step 12.5's record commit.
    - The arms file is BUILT from `abArms[].drops` (round 1, S8): for each closing arm still in `abArms` (an arm in
      `abArmsWithdrawn` does not run), the landed commits of its `drops` — a landed contingency, every
      `wave55 <L> <U>-S1…` commit and the unit — resolved by subject from `git log` and listed in `git log` ORDER, newest
      first (round 2, process-holes M1: `drops` names the set, not the order); its sections and platforms from `abArms`
      (`drop-L6-U2` now includes filter-effects: round 1, S2).
    - Run `bash tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/ab-arms55.sh <arms-file> --dry-run --against <arm.against>`, then
      `--run --against <arm.against>` (`wave55-final` for both closing arms). The runner refuses an `--against` run whose
      CORPUS-launch block names another tree, says "+ uncommitted" or reads MISMATCH / MISSING for an arm's platform
      (round 1, S5; round 2, instrument-teeth S4), and checks each arm's reverses cumulatively (round 2, M1).
    - Every record is written once, after the LAST verified restore: each arm's installed-build block in
      `build-hashes.txt`, `final/ab-<arm>.driver.txt`, and `final/ab-<arm>.txt` (R-a).
    - The read-out is ARM first,
      `node tools/titan/results/wave52-gate/ab-diff.mjs wave55-ab-<arm> wave55-final --threshold 0.002 --platforms <p>`,
      so Δ = final − arm.
    - Read for MAGNITUDE (wave-54 lesson on U3b). A P → f that an arm reveals is named in the PR and queued.
    - Any lane that claims a further mechanism at the gate gets its own arm under the same rules.
    - If L7 U3 was reverted, the arms run by hand under the same record-after-restore rule.
16. **L6 [W1]** (D11): evidence only. It rewrites BOTH embedded IRs (`test-all.sh` syncs the iOS `Resources/` and the
    Android `assets/` copies on every fixture, SKIP_IOS or not): afterwards record both asset sha1s
    (`shasum -a 1 apps/ios-harness/StyleConverterTest/Resources/tmpOutput.json apps/android-harness/app/src/main/assets/tmpOutput.json`)
    in `../wave55-gate/_note.md` and carry them into BACKLOG's wave-56 obligation 0: wave 56's opening witnesses compare
    against them (round 2, process-holes S9).
17. **Snapshot.**
    - `tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/make-snapshot55.mjs` was delivered by the gate-toolkit stage. Its proof D1
      reproduces `corpus-v6-20.json` byte-for-byte from the wave-54 inputs (`toolkit-proofs.txt`).
    - Run it as `DEV_SHA='0d0d3a3d+wave55 (gate tree <sha>)' node tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/make-snapshot55.mjs tools/titan/results/wave55-gate/score-final.movers0005.json <note.txt> tools/titan/results/corpus-v6-21.json --date <YYYY-MM-DD>`
      (`../wave55-gate/toolkit.done.md` §5).
    - Its source is `corpusSnapshotSource`, never the `--movers 0` record; it refuses the latter (`NOT THE 0.005 RECORD`).
    - It refuses until `final/cell-review.json` and a `wave55-final …` installed-build block exist.
    - `artifact` and `reproduce --run-id` = `wave55-final`, checked against `ls tools/titan/runs/`.
    - `installedBuilds` comes from the corpus launch's block; `probeReverts` from `probeDecisions`.
18. **Ship-time truth check** (wave skill Phase 6 ¶4).
    - Every "landed / fixed / not fixed / delivered" sentence in BACKLOG is re-verified against the FINAL tree.
    - Every `path:LINE` is re-resolved to the live line or replaced by its symbol.
    - The pointer audit is re-run, and each cited line is read, not just its file's existence.
    - `grep` for a scratchpad path returns nothing (lane notes' `TREES:` lines excepted: round-1 N6).
    - A scripted multi-file doc edit asserts each replacement applied; a commit message names only what its own
      `git show --stat` shows (wave-54 lesson; round-1 N2).
    - BACKLOG gets §5's hand-offs and the wave-56 obligations; STATUS gets the wave paragraph; doc-staleness is green.
19. **Ship** per wave skill Phase 6: branch `campaign/wave55`, PR to dev, squash-merge, verify `MERGED`, resync.

## 9. Decisions taken against (or beyond) a brief

C1–C15 and D1–D7 are the generator's (`expectations.json` `planDecisions`, rendered verbatim below the table).
- The audit's §8 numbers C1–C14. C15 is its §6 "Readout constraint".
- The collisions X1–X13 of `briefs-audit.md` §5 are settled by C1 (X7, X9, X11, X12, X13), C2 (X10), C3 (X3), C4 (X4, X5,
  X6), C5 (X1, X2) and C6 (X8).
- D8–D20 are this plan's own. They are prose-only: no `expectations.json` field reads them unless the row says so, and a
  later regeneration that encodes one records it in §10.
- D8, D9 and D20 were owed to the generator; the round-1 regeneration applied them (D28), with `plan-prose.py --fill` /
  `--check` and the self-checks and calibrations re-run on the new sha256 (`fix-r1.done.md`).
- D21–D28 are plan-skeptic round 1's (`fix-r1.done.md`); each is encoded in `planDecisions` and in the generator.
- D38–D39 are plan-skeptic rounds 3–4's (`fix-r3.done.md`, `fix-r4.done.md`); each is encoded in `planDecisions` and in
  the probes.

## 10. Decisions during the wave (orchestrator addendum)

1. **[W-L7a] held exactly (2026-10-12, before the landing).** Marker paint reads every value none / normal; the post
   tree bakes 2 roots / 10 runs; the wire differential pre vs post over six sections reads identical 288 · 0 · 0 · 0 · 0
   (verdict exit 0) and fails on demand (exit 5). **L7 U5 lands at seq 9** (`../wave55-gate/_note.md` "[W-L7a]",
   records `../wave55-gate/wl7a/`).

Empty at planning: `wave55-open` reproduced `wave54-final` on every cell and every byte, so nothing is restated.

Each later item:
- is numbered once (a later heading continues the list, never reuses a number);
- names its run, its rule, the picture it looked at, and the regeneration commit;
- is followed in the same commit by `plan-prose.py --fill` and `--check`.

A decision that changes a carrier, a prediction, a floor or a unit enters `plan-build.py` as an APPENDED row of
`probe-decisions/{reverted,restate,contingency}.json` or `holds.json` (committed with the regeneration; the generator refuses
a regeneration that drops a recorded row — D25), or through the generator's own source, never through the JSON. The
`--reverted` / `--restate` / `--contingency` / `--go-small` flags remain for dry runs and self-checks only.
